# THE OFFERING — EP09: She Is Mine

S#1 — VAEL'S KEEP / ONYX HALL / DAIS / CONTINUOUS FROM EP8

[Visual]
The braziers have gone out. The hall is dark except for the gold glow from ISOLDE's wrist marks and the new forming flame-curve on her collarbone. Her hand is still over VAEL's scaled hand at her own collarbone. The third wing-roar has just faded.

VAEL looks at her — closer than the half-inch he held in EP8 S#5. He moves the scaled hand from her collarbone to her jaw. He lifts. He bends. He finishes the line. He does not speak it aloud. He speaks it against her mouth.

VAEL: She is mine.

Then he closes the half-inch. His mouth on hers. Hard. Open. His tongue pushes in immediately. Her lips part for him. She gasps once. He swallows it. The braziers around the hall reignite — all twelve at once. The dark hum becomes gold light.

The court — the twelve armed escorts on their knees, HALDREN on one knee, the captain still standing — sees. ELARA at the back of the hall sees.

He breaks. A half-inch. His scaled thumb rests on her bottom lip.

# THE OFFERING — EP10: Witness

[Visual]
Twelve drakes on the ramparts. Their wings folded but visible. Massive bodies — each as long as the keep wall is high. KIRAN walks between them, calm, hand resting on the shoulder of one as he passes. The drakes do not move when he passes. ISOLDE bends one knee — just one — and lays her hand on the side of the drake's jaw. The drake closes its eyes once. Then opens them. The eleven other drakes lower their heads in sequence. A wave.

The bedchamber. Larger now — moved furniture, low table for war maps in the corner. ISOLDE is dressed in a darker gown — high-collared, but the sleeves are open at the wrist, the three left-wrist marks visible. The new flame-curve on her right collarbone shows above the collar.

VAEL is at the corner table, reading dispatch reports. His scaled hand traces the road on the map. He looks up when she enters. He does not return to the report.

ISOLDE: You said sleep.
VAEL: I said we sleep. Not this hour.
ISOLDE: When.
VAEL: After the first column reaches the river.
ISOLDE: That is tomorrow.
VAEL: Yes.

She crosses to him. She stops at the corner table. Her hand goes to the map — flat, palm down, across the road line.

ISOLDE: Stop them at the river.
VAEL: I plan to.
ISOLDE: Without me at the rampart.
VAEL: With you in the keep.
ISOLDE: They will say you hid me.
VAEL: They will say what they say.

He takes her wrist — gently — and turns her hand off the map. He brings her hand to the side of his face. He holds it there.

VAEL: I have not had a mate for two hundred years.
ISOLDE: That long.
VAEL: Yes.
ISOLDE: Then four days is not very long.

S#2 — VAEL'S KEEP / VAEL'S WING / NIGHT

[Visual]
The bed. They have stopped speaking. VAEL has her gown down to her waist — the bite mark on her right collarbone now matched by his mouth, kissing it slowly. His scaled hand spreads flat across her back — fingers wide. His gauntleted hand at her ribs.

She arches into him. Her own hand goes to the scales of his hand at her back — fingers between the scales. The scaled hand presses harder.

She breaks. Half-inch. Her breath at his ear.

ISOLDE: Not the first night.
VAEL: No.
ISOLDE: But.
VAEL: But this.

He bends his head. His mouth at her left wrist — at the stacked marks + flame-curve. He licks once. The flame-curve flares. Her body responds — she pushes against him.

He breaks his mouth from her wrist. He looks at her.

VAEL: When we sleep — really sleep — it will not be like this.
ISOLDE: How then.
VAEL: Different.

S#3 — VAEL'S KEEP / RAMPART / DEEP NIGHT

[Visual]
ISOLDE alone on the rampart. Dressed again, dark cloak over the gown. She watches the road. The dust column has stopped — the brother's army has made camp. The five fires are scattered now — many fires. Forty-eight of them, by KIRAN's count.

Carrow lands beside her, silent. ISOLDE does not turn.

ISOLDE: He is asleep.
CARROW (low chuff, recognizable as agreement)
ISOLDE: He has not slept in days.

She reaches into the lining of her cloak — the place where the pendant has lived since EP1. She brings the pendant out. She looks at it. The sigil on it has dimmed in the days since EP3 — the sigil pulse, audience-only, no longer matches the rune above the gate as cleanly as it did.

ISOLDE: Why.

Carrow noses at the pendant. Carrow's gold trace at the jaw — the same gold as ISOLDE's wrist marks — touches the pendant. The pendant's sigil pulses once, bright. Then fades again.

ISOLDE: There is something.

She closes her hand on the pendant. She puts it back in the lining.

S#4 — KINGDOM ROAD / BROTHER'S CAMP / DEEP NIGHT

[Visual]
A larger camp than expected. Many tents. A central tent — larger, dark-colored, no banner. Inside: a man at a table. Older than VAEL would be in human years but his face is unlined — like he has held youth. His name is unspoken. He reads a letter sealed in the same blacker seal as the second writ at EP6.

He smiles. Just slightly.

He sets the letter down. He lifts a small object from a velvet cloth — a stone amulet, dark, the size of a thumbprint. He turns it. On its underside: the same sigil as ISOLDE's mother's pendant. The same crest as on the assassin's collar.

He closes his hand on the amulet.

# THE OFFERING — EP13: The River

S#1 — VALLEY / OAKWATER RIVER / DAWN

[Visual]
A wide shallow river. The brother's army on the far bank — two hundred and seventy mounted. Forty-eight in lighter armor at the front, longer spears. The lead among the forty-eight is older, scarred face — the same lead scout from EP11. The dust has settled. They are formed in a wedge facing the keep side.

On the keep side: the keep's wall garrison plus twelve drakes. Carrow at the rear. The other eleven in a curved arc — wing-tips touching. VAEL stands at the center of the arc, on foot, scaled hand open.

ISOLDE is not present.

LEAD SCOUT (across the river): Yield her.
VAEL: She is not yours.
LEAD SCOUT: She is not yours either.
VAEL: She is herself.

The lead scout raises a hand. The forty-eight spears come level.

VAEL: Last word.
LEAD SCOUT: Yield her.

VAEL does not answer. He lowers his scaled hand to the river. The water touches his scales. He turns his palm up. The river ripples backward — against the current — toward the brother's army's side.

S#2 — OAKWATER RIVER / CONTINUOUS

[Visual]
The river runs backward. Against the current. A wave rises against the brother's army's bank — small at first, then taller. The forty-eight spear-front breaks formation as horses shy. The lead scout shouts orders.

The eleven drakes lift off — one wing-beat each, synchronized. They are over the river in three beats. Carrow lifts from the rear, behind them.

The forty-eight drake-trained men set their spears. The first wave of drakes — six of them — dive low. The forty-eight loose their spears at the dive. Three drakes turn the spears with wing-edges. Three take the spears in the shoulder — minor wounds. The drakes' jaws open. Fire — short controlled bursts — onto the forty-eight's spear-line.

Sixteen of the forty-eight go down in the first pass. Twelve are burned. Four are crushed.

The remaining thirty-two retreat behind the cavalry line.

The drakes loop and turn. They do not pursue further. They return to the keep-side bank.

The lead scout sounds a horn — withdraw. The brother's army falls back from the river.

S#3 — VAEL'S KEEP / VAEL'S WING / EVENING (SAME DAY)

[Visual]
VAEL has returned. He has a long cut on his left arm — gauntleted side. Not deep but bleeding. ISOLDE meets him at the wing door. She looks at the cut. He has not bothered to bandage it.

ISOLDE: How many.
VAEL: Sixteen.
ISOLDE: Theirs.
VAEL: Theirs.
ISOLDE: Yours.
VAEL: Three drakes wounded. Light. Their backs.
ISOLDE: Your arm.
VAEL: Yes.

She takes him inside. She closes the door behind them. She sits him on the bed edge. She brings a basin of water and a strip of dark cloth. She rolls back his gauntleted sleeve.

The cut runs from elbow to wrist — clean, narrow.

She washes it. Slowly. Her fingers on the inside of his forearm — gentle. He watches her face, not the cut.

VAEL: First time.
ISOLDE: First what.
VAEL: First time someone has bound my arm.
ISOLDE: Two hundred years.
VAEL: Yes.

She presses the cloth against the cut. She binds it. She ties it. She holds her hand over the binding for a moment longer than necessary.

ISOLDE: Sleep tonight.
VAEL: Yes.

S#4 — VAEL'S KEEP / VAEL'S WING / DEEP NIGHT

[Visual]
The bed. VAEL asleep — actually asleep for the first time in days. ISOLDE awake. She lies on her side facing him. Her left wrist — the three marks — is between them on the bedding. Audience-only: the marks pulse with each of his breaths. Slow. Synchronized.

[Visual]
HALDREN at the table. KIRAN. VAEL — his cut from yesterday already healed (faster than human). The map shows the brother's camp position — they have not moved. Two days since the river battle.

HALDREN: He healed them.

VAEL: How.

ISOLDE: But the brother does.

ISOLDE: And I.

The table is silent. HALDREN does not answer.

VAEL looks at ISOLDE.

VAEL: That is what the pendant is.
ISOLDE: My mother's.
VAEL: Your mother held the line.

S#3 — VAEL'S KEEP / ISOLDE'S OLD CHAMBER (NOT WING) / EVENING

[Visual]
The smaller chamber from EP3·EP5. ISOLDE has come here alone. She kneels at the foot of the bed. From under the bed she draws a small carved box — the only thing she brought from Velden besides the pendant. She opens it.

Inside: three things. The pendant. A folded piece of parchment, very old. A small dark stone — the same kind of stone as the brother's amulet from EP12, but smaller.

She unfolds the parchment. It is a family genealogy — written in old hand. Names. Generations. The last name on it: her mother's. Then a blank.

She traces her finger up the names. Three generations up — there is a name that has been struck through. Two generations above that — another name struck through.

She closes the parchment. She holds the small dark stone in her palm. It is warm.

ISOLDE (quietly, alone): They sent me to die because I am next.

# THE OFFERING — EP15: Tomorrow Night (HEAVY GATE)

S#1 — VAEL'S KEEP / VAEL'S WING / EVENING

[Visual]
The bedchamber. ISOLDE has bathed. Her hair is loose. She wears a thin under-gown, dark — no jewelry except her own three wrist marks and the two collarbone marks, all five glowing softly. The pendant is set on the corner table beside the carved box from EP14 — she has not put it on tonight.

VAEL enters. He has bathed also. He wears a loose dark shirt and trousers — no court armor. He stops at the door. He looks at her.

VAEL: Tonight is not the night I take you.
ISOLDE: I know.
VAEL: Tonight is the night before.
ISOLDE: I know.

He crosses the room. He stops one pace from her. He lifts her chin with his scaled fingers. He bends. He kisses her — first soft, then opening. His tongue meets hers. Her hands rise to his shoulders.

He breaks. His scaled hand stays at her chin.

VAEL: Lie down.

She lies on the bed. She turns her head to watch him.

[Visual]
VAEL kneels at the bedside again — same position as EP9 S#3. He lays his scaled hand flat on her stomach, over the thin under-gown. He does not slide further. His gauntleted hand finds the hem of the gown at her thigh. He lifts the hem — slowly — to her hip.

Her left leg is bare to the hip. The under-gown's slit runs full length. He looks at her thigh — not at her face. His scaled hand moves from her stomach to the inside of her thigh — high — but settles. He does not move further up.

His gauntleted hand at the same time — finds the top button of the under-gown's collar. He undoes it. Then the next. Then the next. The under-gown opens to her waist.

The bite mark above the collar from EP3. The new bite + new flame-curve on the right collarbone from EP8 + EP9. The wrist marks. All five glowing brighter now — pulsing in time with her breath.

He bends. He kisses the bite mark above her collar. Open-mouthed. Slow. His scaled hand still on the high inside of her thigh — pressing now, lightly.

She makes a small sound. He stops.

VAEL: I will stop when you say.
ISOLDE: I will not say.

He continues. He kisses down — to the right collarbone bite + flame-curve. He licks the flame-curve. It flares bright. He kisses lower — to her sternum. The gauntleted hand opens the gown fully — to her hips. He kisses the soft skin between her breasts.

He does not go lower.

ISOLDE: Where do you stop tonight.
VAEL: Here.

He kisses her sternum once more. He raises his head. His scaled hand stays on her thigh.

# THE OFFERING — EP17: The Stone

S#1 — VAEL'S KEEP / ISOLDE'S OLD CHAMBER / MORNING

[Visual]
ISOLDE alone with the carved box. She holds the small dark stone in her palm. ELARA enters quietly — invited.

ISOLDE: How.
ELARA: I do not know. I was a child.
ISOLDE: Show me what you remember.
ELARA: I remember a sound.

ELARA hums — a low, three-note sequence. The stone in ISOLDE's palm warms.

ISOLDE looks at it. She tries the same three notes. The stone warms more.

She closes her hand on the stone. She closes her eyes. She thinks of her mother.

The stone pulses. ISOLDE flinches — eyes open. The flame-curve on her right collarbone has brightened. Three notes ringing in her own throat without her opening her mouth.

ELARA: It does.

ISOLDE in the tower. The exchange continues — she hears him in her head and speaks back the same way. ELARA at the door watches her but cannot hear.

ISOLDE: I know that.
BROTHER: My brother will not surrender the crown.
ISOLDE: Then he will lose it.
BROTHER: And the army at the river — that army is mine. I called them.
ISOLDE: Then call them off.
BROTHER: I will not. Because if you become queen, you become my queen. I will hold the army at the river until you say yes.
ISOLDE: I will not say yes.
BROTHER: Then I come for you myself. In three days.
ISOLDE: Come.

She releases the stone. Her hands tremble. ELARA crosses the threshold — finally — and kneels beside her.

ISOLDE: He answered.
ELARA: What did he say.
ISOLDE: He comes in three days.

S#3 — VAEL'S KEEP / ONYX HALL / DAIS / CONTINUOUS

[Visual]
The brother lifts his package. He unwraps it. Inside: a small carved object. It is the mirror of ISOLDE's mother's pendant — the same sigil, but inverted. He sets it on the foot of the dais.

BROTHER: This was your mother's twin. It was the queen's marker. Whoever holds both is the queen.
ISOLDE: You held it.
BROTHER: I held it for you.
ISOLDE: And now.
BROTHER: I give it to you. With one condition.

He looks at VAEL.

BROTHER: Send my niece with me. Through the line, she will rule Velden. Her mate stays here. Once a year, she will visit. She will rule the line. He will rule his keep.
VAEL: No.
BROTHER: I was speaking to her.
ISOLDE: He spoke for me.
BROTHER: Then you refuse.
ISOLDE: I refuse the splitting. I take both. He is here. Velden is mine. Both at once.
BROTHER: That is war.
ISOLDE: That was already war when you brought three hundred mounted.
BROTHER: I brought them as honor guard for my queen.
ISOLDE: You brought them because you thought I would say yes.
BROTHER: Yes.
ISOLDE: I do not.

The brother picks up the twin pendant. He weighs it in his hand. Then he sets it down again — at the foot of the dais.

BROTHER: I leave it here. Whether you take it is yours. The line will know.

He turns. He walks out.

ISOLDE: Take your army with you.
BROTHER (without turning): I will leave it at the river. It is not for harm. It is for testimony. When you come for the line in Velden, they will follow you.

He exits.

# THE OFFERING — EP20: Two Crowns (HEAVY GATE)

S#1 — VAEL'S KEEP / VAEL'S WING / EARLY MORNING

[Visual]
ISOLDE wakes. She is on the wide bed. The under-gown is fully off — beside the bed. She lies bare under the dark coverlet. VAEL beside her — same. His scaled hand is at her left wrist. The three crescents + flame-curve glow steady. Last night they went further than EP15 — but he held the last threshold. First night still ahead.

She turns to him. He is awake — watching her again.

ISOLDE: How long this time.
VAEL: Two hours since you slept.
ISOLDE: You watched me all night.
VAEL: I slept three hours.

She rolls toward him. Her bare chest meets his. His scaled arm closes around her back.

ISOLDE: Why did you stop.
VAEL: Because the first night should not be the night before the queen takes Velden.
ISOLDE: You are formal.
VAEL: I am traditional.
ISOLDE: Two hundred years of tradition.
VAEL: Yes.

She presses her forehead to his.

ISOLDE: Today I take Velden.
VAEL: Through the line.
ISOLDE: Through the line.
VAEL: Then through the line tonight.

S#2 — VAEL'S KEEP / TOWER ROOM / MORNING

[Visual]
ISOLDE in the tower. Pendant + twin pendant + stone — all three on the stone floor before her. ELARA at the door. KIRAN at the rampart below. HALDREN in the hall.

ISOLDE places her hand over all three items. She hums the three notes. The items pulse together.

ISOLDE listens. ELARA cannot hear. ISOLDE's expression shifts — she is hearing many voices, the whole line going back.

Then she speaks into the stone.

ISOLDE: I am here. I take the line. I am queen by blood. The mate is here. Witness.

The three items pulse, all together. Then settle.

ISOLDE: The line has heard.

S#3 — VELDEN CITY / OLD PALACE / SIMULTANEOUS

[Visual]
The kingdom king — ALDRIC — finally seen on-screen, directly. Older than VAEL would look. In his throne room. He is alone — court not yet assembled. On the wall above his throne: the kingdom crown, mounted on a small dark cushion. The crown itself is plain dark metal, two-bar simple.

The crown lifts off its cushion. Slowly. It floats.

ALDRIC sees it. He rises from the throne — slow.

The crown floats toward an empty space three paces from the throne — and rests on the empty air at the height of a head that is not there. Hovering.

ALDRIC: Show yourself.

A figure begins to appear under the floating crown — not solid. A projection. ISOLDE. She is wearing the gown she was wearing in the tower. The pendant is at her throat. She is faintly translucent — but recognizable.

ISOLDE (projection): I take the line.
ALDRIC: You die in my brother's keep.
ISOLDE: I live in my mate's keep. I rule Velden through the line.
ALDRIC: You cannot.
ISOLDE: I am.

The crown above her projected head lowers — and sits on her head. The crown is not translucent. It is solid. It has crossed over.

ALDRIC: Bring it back. (to no one — there is no one in the throne room)
ISOLDE: It will not come back.

The projection fades. The crown sits where her head was — hovering. Then it lowers — settles on the empty floor three paces from the throne.

The kingdom crown is no longer on its cushion. The kingdom crown is on the floor.

S#5 — VAEL'S KEEP / VAEL'S WING / NIGHT

[Visual]
The bedchamber. VAEL knows what has happened. He has seen the carved box. He looks at ISOLDE — fully naked again, on the bed. He is on the bed beside her, also fully naked. The five marks all blazing now — paired with three new gold threads at his scaled knuckles + a new one beginning at his wrist.

VAEL: Queen of Velden.
ISOLDE: Mate.
VAEL: Tonight.
ISOLDE: Tonight.

He bends — kisses her with full mouth, full tongue, no holding back. His scaled hand slides down her body — past her sternum, across her stomach, to the inside of her thigh. This time it does not settle. It slides up. The gauntleted hand at her breast.

She arches into him. Her hands fist in his hair. The kiss does not break.

He breaks at last — to look at her.

VAEL: Now.
ISOLDE: Now.

He moves over her. The five marks blaze blinding. The room fills with gold light. Audience-only: outside the window, all twelve drakes on the ramparts close their eyes at once. The night holds.

# THE OFFERING — EP21: Bound

S#1 — VAEL'S KEEP / VAEL'S WING / DAWN

[Visual]
ISOLDE wakes. VAEL is beside her — asleep. Both still naked under the coverlet. The dawn light is just touching the room. ISOLDE turns her head and looks at her left arm.

The three crescents and the flame-curve are still there — burning steady. But a new mark has appeared overnight: a full bracelet around her wrist — gold, dark-edged, complete circle. Mate-seal mark.

She raises her right hand and touches the new bracelet. It pulses warm.

She looks at VAEL. His scaled knuckles — on the bedding between them — show four full gold threads now, also forming a bracelet under the scales.

ISOLDE: Vael.
VAEL (waking slowly): Yes.
ISOLDE: Look.

He looks. He raises his scaled hand. He turns it. The bracelet under the scales is complete.

VAEL: The seal.
ISOLDE: We are sealed.
VAEL: We are.

He brings his scaled hand to her bracelet — palm against wrist. The two bracelets touch. Both pulse — once — bright together.

S#3 — VAEL'S KEEP / ONYX HALL / DAIS / MIDDAY

[Visual]
Council assembled. HALDREN, KIRAN, ELARA, plus three other lieutenants of VAEL. ISOLDE on the high seat. VAEL standing behind. ISOLDE wears the bracelet visible. The Velden crown is not on her head — it is in the carved box.

ISOLDE: My uncle ALDRIC will move within days.
HALDREN: He has already begun. The kingdom council was called at dawn.
ISOLDE: What will he do.
HALDREN: He cannot recall the crown. The line will not give it back. He must hold his throne another way.
KIRAN: Which way.
HALDREN: He will denounce her as the offering — as one no longer human, taken by the dragon. He will call all gift-bearing lines to him.
ISOLDE: How many.

ISOLDE: Will they come.
HALDREN: Some. He has nothing to offer them but blood-right. You have the line and the crown and the mate. They come to you.
ISOLDE: Send word. To each.
HALDREN: Through the line.
ISOLDE: Through the line.

ISOLDE sits on the high seat. Velden crown still in the carved box at her side.

ELDEST: When my grandmother died, this passed to me. I am not strong enough to use it. But it answered when the line called.

ELDEST: They are.
ISOLDE: Will you stand with me.
ELDEST: That is why we came.

The three women kneel — not full subservience, half-knee acknowledgment. ISOLDE places each piece on the dais step.

They rise.

S#4 — VAEL'S KEEP / VAEL'S WING / NIGHT

[Visual]
ISOLDE in the bed reading from a thick old book — the line's records, brought by the eldest carrier. VAEL beside her, reading over her shoulder. Both naked under the coverlet. Bracelet glowing soft.

ISOLDE (reading): "And the first queen was carried by the line into the dragon-bond, and her bond bore the seal of both. The seal is a bracelet around the wrist of the queen, and a bracelet under the scales of the dragon-mate."

She reads the next line silently. Her face changes. She closes the book.

A pause. He takes her hand. The bracelets touch.

VAEL: Whatever it is.
ISOLDE: Whatever it is.

S#2 — DISTANT KINGDOM TOWN / NIGHT (PARALLEL)

[Visual]
A small stone house in a market town. Inside: an old woman — older than the eldest carrier at the keep — sits by a low fire. She holds a small carved box. There is a crash at the door. Six soldiers enter. Bone-clasp leather but with kingdom regular insignia, not the brother's army.

OLD WOMAN: I know why you have come.
LEAD SOLDIER: My king commands the line.
OLD WOMAN: My queen called it home.
LEAD SOLDIER: There is no queen.
OLD WOMAN: There is one. You are looking at her granddaughter.

She does not rise. She does not resist. The lead soldier looks at her carved box. He moves toward it. The old woman closes her eyes.

The box on her lap pulses once — bright. The soldier reaches for it. He freezes — hand mid-air. He cannot move closer.

OLD WOMAN: It will not come with you.

The lead soldier raises his sword. He brings it down on the box.

The box shatters. The contents — a pendant, a stone — fly across the room. The pendant lands at the soldier's foot. He picks it up. He drops it instantly — burned.

The stone lands at the old woman's feet. She picks it up. She holds it in her palm.

OLD WOMAN: Tell him I did not give it up.

The lead soldier raises his sword again — toward her this time.

# THE OFFERING — EP27: The Court-Magus

S#1 — VAEL'S KEEP / ONYX HALL / DAIS / MIDDAY

[Visual]
A single hooded rider arrives at the keep — unannounced, alone. He carries the kingdom's blacker seal. KIRAN admits him reluctantly. The figure stops at the foot of the dais. He pulls back the hood.

The court-magus from EP23. Older than VAEL's keep age, but human-old, not unlined.

COURT-MAGUS: My king sends terms.
ISOLDE: I have given terms. He refused them.
COURT-MAGUS: New terms.
ISOLDE: Speak.

He raises a small bundle. He sets it on the dais step. He unwraps it. A withered branch — once silver. The line tree.

COURT-MAGUS: He has cut the tree of the line.
ISOLDE (rising): The —
COURT-MAGUS: The first tree. From the first queen.

She crosses down to the step. She picks up the branch. It is dead. Cold.

ISOLDE: When.
COURT-MAGUS: Three nights ago. After he burned the carriers, he turned to the tree.
ISOLDE: The line still answers.
COURT-MAGUS: The line still answers for now. The tree dies — the line will dim, then fade. Over years.
ISOLDE: Then I will replant.
COURT-MAGUS: The seed is in my keeping.

He takes from his cloak — a small wooden box. Inside: a single seed, gold-veined.

# THE OFFERING — EP29: Aldric Moves

S#1 — VELDEN CITY / OLD PALACE / DAWN

[Visual]
ALDRIC alone. The court-magus is gone. His advisors are reduced — two remain. The kingdom crown is still not on his head. He sits on the throne. Across the room, a tall figure enters — a man in dark robes, older even than ALDRIC. His face cannot be seen — hooded.

DARK ROBE: My king. The line has chosen.
ALDRIC: It has not chosen.
DARK ROBE: It has chosen. The tree has been replanted.
ALDRIC: Then we burn it.
DARK ROBE: The tree was planted by a queen. The tree cannot be burned by other than her hand.
ALDRIC: Then her hand burns it.
DARK ROBE: How.
ALDRIC: She has a price. She has felt it.
DARK ROBE: You knew.
ALDRIC: I knew when her mother held the line. Her mother carried the price too. Her mother is dead.
DARK ROBE: My king. How did you know.
ALDRIC: Because I killed her mother. With my own hand. When her mother declared.
DARK ROBE: I see.

A long pause. The dark robe steps closer.

DARK ROBE: Then we use the price.

# THE OFFERING — EP30: March on Velden (HEAVY GATE)

S#1 — VALLEY ROAD / DAWN

[Visual]
The combined force on the road. Three hundred minus eighteen plus the keep garrison plus twelve drakes plus the line carriers (those who can ride or be flown). At the front: ISOLDE on Carrow, crown on her head, the line tree's first staff (a branch given to her by the tree) in her right hand. VAEL on his fifth drake. BROTHER at her flank.

Behind them: the brother's army. Behind them: the wider valley drakes — forty-eight of them — flying high above. KIRAN coordinates from a drake at the flank. HALDREN at the rear with the line carriers.

LEAD SCOUT (riding up): Two hours to Velden.

S#2 — VELDEN CITY / SOUTH GATE / MID-MORNING

[Visual]
The kingdom army at the south gate — assembled in defense. The kingdom crown still absent from ALDRIC's head. The dark robe figure beside ALDRIC on the city wall. The kingdom army numbers about one thousand foot, two hundred mounted — a city garrison, not field army.

ISOLDE rides into the open space before the gate. She does not approach within bow range — but close enough to be heard.

S#3 — VELDEN CITY / SOUTH GATE / CONTINUOUS

[Visual]
The dark light from the dark robe shoots toward ISOLDE — across the open ground. It moves slow, deliberate, targeting her bracelets directly.

The bracelets blaze in defense. Carrow rears. ISOLDE raises the line tree branch — she points it.

The dark light hits the bracelets. ISOLDE's body convulses — she clutches the staff. The bracelets fight back — gold against dark.

VAEL drives his drake forward. He places his own scaled hand on the dark light beam. His scales burn red — the dark light fights his hand. He keeps it there.

The bracelets and Vael's hand together push the dark light back.

But the dark light has touched ISOLDE. A new mark — black — appears beside the bracelets. Small. Like a stain.

DARK ROBE: She has the stain now.

ISOLDE looks at the stain on her wrist. The bracelets glow brighter — pushing the stain. The stain shrinks but does not disappear.

ISOLDE (loud, to the kingdom army): I am the line. Your king killed my mother. He is no longer king. Yield.

A long silence. Then — the kingdom mounted captain (different from the captain in EP7) steps his horse forward — out of formation. He raises one fist — empty.

KINGDOM MOUNTED CAPTAIN: I yield.

A second captain raises his fist. Then a third. Then half the army. Then most.

The dark robe figure turns. He walks back into the city. ALDRIC stands on the wall alone.

ISOLDE: Aldric. Walk down.
ALDRIC: Niece — the price is taking you.
ISOLDE: It is taking. I am still here.
ALDRIC: For now.
ISOLDE: Walk down.

He does not. He turns. He walks back into the city after the dark robe.

# THE OFFERING — EP31: Inside Velden

S#1 — VELDEN CITY / MAIN STREET / AFTERNOON

[Visual]
ISOLDE rides Carrow at walking pace through the city. The people line the streets. They do not cheer. They watch. Some have heard ALDRIC's confession at the gate — the message has spread. Others have not. They all see the crown, the bracelets, the dragon.

A small child runs from the crowd — eight or nine, dirty, no parent visible. She holds a small wilted flower. She offers it.

ISOLDE dismounts. She kneels. She takes the flower. The flower revives in her hand — gold-edged.

CHILD: My grandmother said you would come back.
ISOLDE: When did she say.
CHILD: When the dragons came.
ISOLDE: Where is she.
CHILD: She died last year.
ISOLDE: What did she look like.
CHILD: She had a mark like yours.

ISOLDE looks at the child's wrist. A small faint gold mark — the line. ISOLDE places her hand over it. The mark warms.

ISOLDE: What is your name.
CHILD: Wren.
ISOLDE: Walk with me.

# THE OFFERING — EP32: Throne Room

S#1 — VELDEN CITY / OLD PALACE / THRONE ROOM / EVENING

[Visual]
ISOLDE crosses to the foot of the throne. VAEL beside her. BROTHER and KIRAN at the door. The dark robe begins to step backward — toward a hidden door behind the throne.

ISOLDE points the line tree staff at ALDRIC.

ISOLDE: You killed my mother. You took the crown that did not belong to you. You burned five of the line. You sent assassins. You tried to kill me through the dark.
ALDRIC: All of it.
ISOLDE: Confess it in writing.
ALDRIC: Why.
ISOLDE: Because Velden needs to know.
ALDRIC: Velden will know.
ISOLDE: In writing. Now.

She points the staff at a small writing desk by the throne. The desk slides forward — magical movement. Parchment and quill rise into position.

ALDRIC: I refuse.
ISOLDE: The line takes from you if you refuse.

ALDRIC looks at the desk. He looks at the staff. The dark robe is still moving backward — slow, watching.

ALDRIC: Write what.
ISOLDE: Truth.

ALDRIC rises. He moves to the desk. He picks up the quill. He writes.

S#1 — VAEL'S KEEP / WAR ROOM / DAY

[Visual]
Map of the kingdom. Pins mark the locations of old line carriers (now mostly safe at the keep) and the locations of known old growths of the original line tree (twelve sites — one of which they destroyed at the northern temple).

ISOLDE: Then we secure them all.

COURT-MAGUS: They are still there.
ISOLDE: Three generations later.
COURT-MAGUS: The line keeps them.
ISOLDE: Then they come to me, or I go to them.

ISOLDE: Take a drake escort. Bring them back if they wish — protect the groves either way.
BROTHER: Yes.

GROVE KEEPER: I have been here forty years. My mother sixty before me. We have never left this grove.

The woman mounts behind a drake rider. The child she carries.

ISOLDE: He found her.

ISOLDE: How did he reach her before we did.
KIRAN: She was very old. Eighty. She did not move quickly.
ISOLDE: Was she alone.
KIRAN: She lived alone.
COURT-MAGUS: He has dead pieces from her tree. He is rebuilding.

A silence.

ISOLDE: I should have moved faster.
VAEL: We could not be everywhere.
ISOLDE: The stain wins when I am slow.

She looks at her wrist. The bracelets are still bright but she is paler today.

ISOLDE: We hunt him now.

# THE OFFERING — EP38: Inside the Wards

S#1 — HILL FORT / INNER COURTYARD / AFTERNOON

[Visual]
ISOLDE walks toward the counter-pole. VAEL behind her. The acolytes form a circle. The dark robe stands at the pole.

DARK ROBE: You came alone through the wards.
ISOLDE: I came with my mate.
DARK ROBE: One drake-mate against four mages and me.
ISOLDE: And the staff.
DARK ROBE: And the staff.

He raises his hands. The dark light pulses inward from the wards — now turned toward her. ISOLDE plants the staff. Gold blazes from the staff base. Gold against dark. The four acolytes chant. The pull is hard.

ISOLDE: Vael.
VAEL: Beside you.

He places his scaled hand on the staff alongside hers. His scales burn. He adds his strength. The gold pushes harder. The dark light pushes back.

The dark robe steps forward.

DARK ROBE: Together you are stronger. Apart you are bound.

He raises a hand toward VAEL. Dark light shoots at VAEL specifically. ISOLDE pivots — places her bracelets between VAEL and the dark light. The dark light hits her bracelets. ISOLDE staggers.

But the dark light has not reached VAEL. The stain on his scaled hand actually shrinks a fraction — pushed back by her bracelets' impact.

S#2 — HILL FORT / INNER COURTYARD / CONTINUOUS

[Visual]
ISOLDE steadies herself. The bracelets are darker now — more stain. But VAEL is safer. The dark robe is enraged.

DARK ROBE: You traded your life for his moment of safety.
ISOLDE: I will trade more.
DARK ROBE: How much.
ISOLDE: Until you are dead.

She raises the staff. She points it at the acolytes — not at the dark robe.

The staff fires gold light at the four acolytes. The four chant harder — they resist. But their resistance breaks one by one. Each falls in turn — not dead, just unconscious, their dark connection severed.

Four down. Only the dark robe remains.

The dark robe pulls another flame from his sleeve. He marks the floor — portal again.

DARK ROBE: I will be back.
VAEL: How many times must we have this conversation.
DARK ROBE: One more.

He steps through. ISOLDE attempts to follow — Vael catches her arm — she is too weak now.

ISOLDE in bed. Unconscious. VAEL beside her — not asleep. COURT-MAGUS examining the bracelets. ELARA at the bed foot.

ALDRIC: With what.
DARK ROBE: With a curse on my blood. A counter-line curse. When the line tries to take more from me — it cannot. The curse rebounds. The line dies in its source. The queen dies.

ALDRIC sits up. He looks at the door — locked from outside, but he is unguarded inside.

S#4 — VAEL'S KEEP / VAEL'S WING / NIGHT

[Visual]
ISOLDE and VAEL in bed. Both clothed in nightwear. ISOLDE traces the dark patch on VAEL's scaled hand with her finger. It is smaller than before.

ISOLDE: Yours is fading.
VAEL: Mine is fading.
ISOLDE: Mine is too.
VAEL: When the dark robe is gone — they may fade entirely.
ISOLDE: Or they may stay.
VAEL: We do not know.

She lays her head on his chest.

ISOLDE: I have not asked you something important.
VAEL: Ask.
ISOLDE: You have lived two hundred years. You have not had a mate.
VAEL: No.
ISOLDE: Did you want one.
VAEL: I wanted whichever the line sent.
ISOLDE: That is not the same as wanting.
VAEL: I am older now than you will ever be. I have learned to wait for what the line decides. When you arrived — I knew the line decided.
ISOLDE: Before the marks.
VAEL: Before the marks.

A pause.

ISOLDE: Vael.
VAEL: Yes.
ISOLDE: When I die.
VAEL: When.
ISOLDE: Not now. Eventually.
VAEL: Yes.
ISOLDE: Live afterward.
VAEL: I will not.
ISOLDE: Vael.
VAEL: I have decided. The bond would not let me live alone afterward anyway. But I would not even if it would.
ISOLDE: That is sad.
VAEL: It is true.

She closes her eyes against his chest.

He pulls her to him. He holds her — long. He does not kiss her.

S#3 — MOUNTAIN SHRINE / NIGHT

The dark robe raises his hands. He pulls the dead piece from his cloak. He holds it forward.

DARK ROBE: This was the last queen before yours. Before your mother. Before your grandmother. The line of the queen who started the kingdom — buried for two hundred years. I have it.

He drives the piece into the counter-pole. The counter-pole flares dark — much darker than at the temple or the hill fort. This piece is more powerful.

# THE OFFERING — EP44: The Dark Robe Falls

S#1 — MOUNTAIN SHRINE / DEEP NIGHT

[Visual]
The gold light has filled the shrine. The eight acolytes are unconscious — collapsed in their circle. The counter-pole is destroyed — split down the center by ISOLDE's staff. The dead piece inside is dissolving — gold absorbing it.

The dark robe is on his knees in the gold light. His hood has fallen back. He is older than expected — perhaps eighty in human years. His skin has faint gold-line markings — he was once line, before he turned.

ISOLDE walks to him.

ISOLDE: Who were you.
DARK ROBE: I was — the carrier who chose the dark when my queen died. Two hundred years ago.
ISOLDE: Why.
DARK ROBE: She was my mate. She died from the price. I have lived since then, trying to break the line so no other queen would carry the price.
ISOLDE: That is grief.
DARK ROBE: That is grief.

A long silence. ISOLDE looks at him. She does not strike. The granddaughter at her side watches.

ISOLDE: Why did you kill my mother's grandmothers.
DARK ROBE: To stop the line. So the price would not take another queen.
ISOLDE: I will pay the price gladly.
DARK ROBE: You will.
ISOLDE: But I will live.
DARK ROBE: For a time.

ISOLDE crouches. She lays her hand on his shoulder. Gold passes from her into him. The dark in him fades. The gold lines on his skin brighten — he is line again.

He looks up.

DARK ROBE: You took my dark.
ISOLDE: The line took your dark. The line returns.
DARK ROBE: She loved me.
ISOLDE: I know.

He bows his head. He stops moving. After a moment, he is still. Two hundred years of grief released. He has died — peacefully, line returned to him.

ISOLDE — at the palace doors — emerges. She wears a queen's dress — dark with gold-threaded sigil. The Velden crown is in her hands, not yet on her head. She walks slowly toward the tree.

VAEL emerges behind her. He wears formal dark robes — no court armor. He walks one pace behind her.

The crowd is silent.

S#3 — VELDEN CITY / GREAT SQUARE / LINE TREE / CONTINUOUS

[Visual]
The roars fade. The crowd remains kneeling. ISOLDE turns to VAEL.

He walks to stand at her right side. She turns to the crowd.

ISOLDE: This is my mate. He is named Mate-King. He rules with me. Both keeps are ours. Velden city is ours. The line is mine. The dragon-bond is his. Together we hold the kingdom.

The crowd remains kneeling.

ISOLDE: And I will name what we pay for this crown.

A silence — anticipating.

ISOLDE: The line carries a price. The price is years. I will not see eighty. I will see perhaps fifty. Perhaps less. Velden knows this. Velden chose me knowing this.

The crowd murmurs — not in disagreement. In understanding.

ISOLDE: My mate — he carries some of the price with me. He will live longer than I will. But the bond will not let him live past me. We die together. Whenever the day comes.

VAEL bows his head once — public acknowledgment.

ISOLDE: Until that day — we rule.

The crowd rises in waves. One by one. They do not cheer. They simply rise. The acknowledgment is the rising.

[Visual]

S#2 — VELDEN CITY / GREAT SQUARE / MORNING

[Visual]
The square fills again. Word has spread — the Mate-King will show himself. The crowd is bigger than yesterday's coronation. The southern lords return — this time in armor, allowed inside the city as honorary guard.

The carriers form their circle around the tree. The drakes — sixty visible — wait on every rampart and rooftop. KIRAN coordinates positions.

ISOLDE arrives at the tree's foot. VAEL beside her. The crowd is silent.

He raises his scaled hand. The bracelet under the scales blazes. The paired threads with ISOLDE's marks blaze in answer.

S#3 — VELDEN CITY / GREAT SQUARE / LINE TREE / MIDDAY

[Visual]
The sun reaches midday. VAEL stands in the clear space. He closes his eyes. His body changes — slow at first.

His skin darkens. Scales bloom across his shoulders, down his back, along his arms. The gauntleted hand becomes fully scaled. His hair extends into a longer, thicker dark mane. His eyes — when he opens them — are fully non-human now. The pupils vertical.

He continues. He grows taller. His shoulders broaden. From his back — slowly — wings unfold. Massive. Black with crimson under-leather. They open fully — at full extension, they span twice the width of the line tree.

His mouth opens. Now there are no human teeth — pointed. A long muzzle has begun to form. His face has shifted. He is no longer fully human-shaped.

He grows further. The crowd has stepped back further. The clear space has become much larger. He is now at perhaps thirty feet tall — too large for human form but not yet full drake.

He pauses there.

VAEL (his voice deeper, layered with a roaring undertone): This is half.
ISOLDE: Half.
VAEL: This is what I show.

He continues. His shoulders narrow into a serpentine torso. His legs become full hind legs of a drake. The wings widen further. He grows taller still — until he is at full drake form, larger than Carrow, larger than any drake on the ramparts.

He is fully a dragon now. The line tree at his side. ISOLDE at his foreleg.

The square is silent. The southern lords have all knelt — without orders. The drakes around the city — all sixty — have lowered their heads. The carriers have lowered their heads too.

VAEL (now in drake-voice — half roar half words): The bond is dragon. The crown is queen. The line is ours.

He extends one massive wing over ISOLDE — protective canopy.

ISOLDE looks up at him. She reaches and lays her hand on his foreleg. Her palm against his scales.

ISOLDE: Mate.
VAEL (drake): Mate.

# THE OFFERING — EP50: The Crowned Bride (FINALE HEAVY GATE)

S#1 — VELDEN CITY / OLD PALACE / QUEEN'S CHAMBER / DAWN (DAY AFTER VAEL'S TRANSFORMATION)

[Visual]
ISOLDE awake — early light. She is in the wide bed alone. VAEL is gone — she can hear him in the next chamber, moving. She places her hand on her stomach. Audience-only: under her palm, a faint new gold pulse — small, deep, layered with both her gold and Vael's red-gold scaled gold. Two-toned. The seed of the line's continuation.

She smiles — small.

VAEL enters with a small bowl of warm food.

VAEL: You are awake.
ISOLDE: I am.
VAEL: How.
ISOLDE: How am I awake.
VAEL: How is — anything.
ISOLDE: I am pregnant.
VAEL: Yes.
ISOLDE: You knew.
VAEL: I felt the third pulse last night.
ISOLDE: Will it survive the price.
VAEL: The line carries through. The mate-bond shares the carrying. Our child will live.
ISOLDE: Then there is a successor.
VAEL: There is.

S#2 — VELDEN CITY / GREAT SQUARE / LINE TREE / MIDDAY

[Visual]
The final coronation ceremony. The full court of Velden + Vael's keep + southern lords + twenty-five carriers + sixty drakes + thousands of citizens. The line tree-crown — gold-leafed — on ISOLDE's head. The Velden crown — the original kingdom crown — held on a cushion at the foot of the line tree.

ISOLDE picks it up. She holds both crowns — the new gold living one and the old metal one.

ISOLDE: The kingdom's old crown was my uncle's. My grandfather's. His grandfather's. They held it without the line. I do not need it.

She places the old crown at the base of the line tree. The tree absorbs it — the metal dissolves into the gold-blazing earth at the tree's foot. Gone.

ISOLDE: From now — only the line tree crown. Only what the line accepts.

She turns to the crowd. She lays her hand on her stomach.

ISOLDE: And there is a successor.

The crowd murmurs — soft, joyful.

ISOLDE: My child will be queen or king after me — and Vael's child after him.

VAEL: Our child.
ISOLDE: Our child.

She turns to VAEL.

ISOLDE: Mate-King. Vael of the keep. King of Velden. Father of the future line. Will you bind to me — as I bind to you — for as long as we live.
VAEL: I will.
ISOLDE: And after.
VAEL: And after.

She places her hand on his chest — over his heart. He places his scaled hand on hers — over her stomach.

The line tree glows — bright, sustained. The crown on her head glows. The bracelets on her wrist glow. The paired threads under his scales glow. All in time.

S#3 — VELDEN CITY / GREAT SQUARE / LINE TREE / AFTERNOON

[Visual]
The crowd has begun the final witnessing — each citizen, each lord, each carrier in turn comes to the tree and places their hand on the bark. The tree pulses with each touch. Each person leaves a small gold thread of their own — a personal contribution to the line.

The line tree grows visibly larger as the day proceeds. By the end of the witnessing — by evening — the tree is significantly taller than it was at dawn. Its branches arch over the entire square. Gold leaves shimmer in the late sun.

ISOLDE and VAEL remain at the base — accepting each witness. The carriers — twenty-five plus the granddaughter — remain in their circle. The drakes — sixty — remain on the ramparts and rooftops.

KIRAN (at sunset, after the last witness): It is done.
ISOLDE: It is done.
VAEL: It is.
KIRAN: My queen. My king.
ISOLDE: My friend.

She turns to him. She removes the gold-leaf circlet briefly. She touches one of the smallest gold leaves to KIRAN's forehead — leaving a faint gold mark there.

ISOLDE: You are line now too.
KIRAN: My queen — I am not blood.
ISOLDE: The line is not blood. The line is who chooses it.

KIRAN places one hand on his forehead — feels the mark. He looks at it.

KIRAN: Yes, my queen.

S#4 — VELDEN CITY / OLD PALACE / GARDEN / NIGHT

[Visual]
ISOLDE and VAEL in the small garden behind the palace — where ALDRIC is buried. They have left the public witnessing. The carriers, drakes, crowd are at the square — the witnessing continues into the night.

ISOLDE sits on a stone bench. VAEL beside her. The line tree visible from here — its glow lights the garden.

ISOLDE: Tomorrow we return to the keep.
VAEL: For a few days.
ISOLDE: Then back to Velden. Or — the keep. Wherever the line takes us.
VAEL: Both.
ISOLDE: Both.

A long pause.

ISOLDE: My uncle.
VAEL: He is part of the line now.
ISOLDE: Forgiven.
VAEL: Returned.
ISOLDE: There is a difference.
VAEL: There is.

She places her hand on the small gold pulse on her stomach.

ISOLDE: When the child comes.
VAEL: When.
ISOLDE: We tell her about him.
VAEL: Or him.
ISOLDE: Or him. Either way.
VAEL: We tell.

A small smile.

ISOLDE: This is our beginning.
VAEL: This is our beginning.

He pulls her against him on the bench. She leans into his chest. The line tree's gold light over the garden. The drakes on the rooftops behind them. The carriers in the square continuing the witnessing.

She closes her eyes.

He looks up at the line tree — at the stars beyond it.

VAEL (softly, alone — almost to himself): The bond holds. The line holds. The queen holds.

He looks down at her — sleeping against his chest.

VAEL: All of it holds.

The line tree pulses one last time — long and slow and sustained. The drakes around the city pulse their gold traces in answer. The carriers in the square pulse their wrist marks. ISOLDE's bracelets glow steady against VAEL's chest. The third pulse — the child — glows under her hand on her stomach.

VAEL closes his eyes too. Just for a moment. Then he opens them. He keeps watching her. He keeps watching the line tree. He keeps watching the future.

The camera draws back. The garden becomes small. The line tree becomes tall against the night sky. The witnessing of Velden continues in the gold light beyond the garden wall — gold threads rising from each carrier's hand on the bark, each small contribution joining the tree.

A wind moves through the garden — soft, the same low wind that crossed the gate at the beginning. It carries the sigil hum that audience first heard at Vael's Keep — but quieter now, settled. The hum that was the line calling itself home.

The wind passes. The garden holds. The line tree glows. ISOLDE sleeps against VAEL. VAEL watches. The witnessing continues.

The image holds.

The image holds.

The image holds.

Fade Out.
